### drain-upgrade-restart — Drain-then-restart as a state machine, so shipped fixes stop being inert

- classes: version drift and upgrade, executor process lifecycle; estimate 30 min; depends on: boot-adopt-executors, watchdog-dispatch-liveness
- problem: rebootApp (src/main/index.cjs:311) kills PTYs and watchers, spawns npx, waits 400 ms and calls app.exit(0) with no read of the queue, no running-count check and no pause — with 4 rows running it orphans four executors, and it races the single-instance lock (the child can lose the lock and exit, leaving the app down until the watchdog's stale branch fires). The only automatic relaunch is crash-triggered and always resolves npm's `latest`, never repo HEAD. So every fix in this chain stays inert until a human restarts at exactly the right moment, which is the historically dangerous act.
- mechanism (as synthesized): New `src/main/lib/upgradeDrain.cjs`: `requestRestart({reason, requestedBy})` writes restart-request.json under schedulerHome(); PURE `evaluateDrain({request, queueSnapshot, identity, now, deadlineMs})` → {action: 'none'|'pause'|'wait'|'restart'|'abort'}. On a pending request it calls the EXISTING setPaused('drain-for-upgrade') so tickQueue's existing paused guard stops dispatch with no new gate; it then WAITS while any row is running or investigating; only at zero does it emit 'restart'. A deadlineMs (default 4 h, matching the observed 240-min max run) converts to 'abort', which clears the pause through the existing clearPause — never a timed kill, never a requeue. Evaluated from the existing 60 s heartbeat interval (no new driver), with the drain state emitted into the heartbeat so the starvation classifier, health and the watchdog do not read a deliberate drain as a stall. rebootApp becomes a thin requestRestart() wrapper; the exit happens from the evaluator, releasing the single-instance lock before exit and handing the child SM_AWAIT_LOCK_MS so terminateLosingInstance retries instead of exiting on first loss. watchdogHelpers gains `maybeRequestUpgradeRestart({buildStatusPath})` on its ALIVE branch: when build-status.json (written by a 6-hourly checkPublishedLatest() in main, replacing the dead src/renderer/lib/updateCheck.ts, which is deleted) shows publishedLatest !== runn
- files: src/main/lib/upgradeDrain.cjs, src/main/index.cjs, src/main/scheduler.cjs, src/main/lib/singleInstanceGuard.cjs, src/main/lib/watchdogHelpers.cjs, scripts/scheduler-watchdog.cjs, src/renderer/lib/updateCheck.ts, src/main/lib/__tests__/upgradeDrain.test.cjs

### cwd-classify-failclosed — One fail-closed cwd classifier: an unprovable worktree is never an ordinary project

- classes: path/project resolution and hot-path cost, state durability; estimate 30 min; depends on: test-sandbox-guard, path-literal-lint
- problem: Three modules each re-implement 'what project is this path' and each fails OPEN. activeSessions.worktreeMainRootOf (src/main/lib/activeSessions.cjs:101) returns null when a worktree's `.git` file survives but `<main>/.git/worktrees/<name>/gitdir` is gone — and projectRootOf (:163) then returns the WORKTREE path unchanged, while ephemeralCwd.isLinkedWorktreeRoot (src/main/lib/ephemeralCwd.cjs:57) returns false for the same reason, so opsOwnership.resolveOpsRoot happily builds <worktree>/session-manager-operations and writeSplit materialises a shard there. Our own code opens that door: gitWorktree runs `git worktree prune` in five places, which removes the admin dir. The two normalizers also disagree: activeSessions uses `parts.indexOf(OPS_DIRNAME)` while opsOwnership.parseOpsPath (:105) and assertOpsWrite (:181) use `segs.lastIndexOf(OPS_ROOT_DIR)`, so for a nested stray ops root the disc
- mechanism (as synthesized): Add `classifyCwd(cwd)` → {kind: 'project'|'worktree'|'ephemeral'|'unknown', projectRoot, reason} as the single normalizer, and route activeSessions.projectRootOf, ephemeralCwd.isEphemeralCwd and opsOwnership.resolveProjectRoot through it. Key change: a directory whose `.git` is a FILE is classified 'worktree' even when the round-trip verification fails — today both call sites treat that as an ordinary project. 'unknown' is non-registrable and non-writable for ops state (dispatch is NOT blocked — refusing writes is the safe half; blocking dispatch would trade one incident class for a worse one). Unify on lastIndexOf(OPS_ROOT_DIR) and delete activeSessions' divergent indexOf. Two cheap hardening items ride along: return a frozen copy from the cwdScanCache (activeSessions.cjs:59) and queueStore's cwdCache so a caller can never mutate a shared array, and raise CACHE_TTL_MS above the poll cadence (POLL_MIN_INTERVAL_MS is 90_000 while both caches are 30_000, so today every tick pays a cold scan) with bustProjectCwdCache wired to epicMint and prdCreate so a brand-new project is still visible immediately. scripts/hooks/guard-inline-implementation.cjs normalizes its agent-supplied rawCwd through the same classifier.
- files: src/main/lib/activeSessions.cjs, src/main/lib/ephemeralCwd.cjs, src/main/lib/opsOwnership.cjs, src/main/lib/queueStore.cjs, src/main/lib/epicMint.cjs, src/main/lib/prdCreate.cjs, scripts/hooks/guard-inline-implementation.cjs, src/main/lib/__tests__/cwdClassify.test.cjs

### prd-gate-spec-shadow — Parse the PRD's own acceptance gate and run it in shadow mode

- classes: post-run adjudication; estimate 30 min; depends on: test-sandbox-guard
- problem: The scheduler decides 'is this PRD done?' by pattern-matching the model's transcript, and never executes the PRD's own machine-checkable gate. The primitive exists (extractAcCommand / reverifyAc in src/main/lib/definitionOfDone.cjs) but is reachable only from runDefinitionOfDoneOnDrain, whose output goes to writeReport and never changes a job status. Worse, when an author DOES write an explicit gate, `SHELL_META_RE = /[|>&;<`]|\$[({]/` (definitionOfDone.cjs:98) rejects any `&&`-chained command, so 11 of the 23 PRDs using the `Gate (… must be green)` convention parse to null — including live row 1218-fo-01, whose gate is `timeout 300 npm run typecheck && timeout 300 npm run lint && timeout 600 npm run test:unit`.
- mechanism (as synthesized): New `src/main/lib/artifactGate.cjs`, mirroring definitionOfDone.cjs's no-shell posture. `extractGateSpec(prdBody, frontmatter)` → {commands: [{argv, timeoutMs}], source} with priority: frontmatter `gate:` list → a fenced ```gate block → the existing `- [ ] Gate (last thing the run does, must be green): \`…\`` AC line SPLIT ON `&&` into a command sequence (this alone un-breaks the 11 PRDs that already author a gate) → fallback to today's extractAcCommand → 'none' (with `gate: none` as an explicit, recorded opt-out for doc-only PRDs). `runGate({cwd, spec, runDir, slug})` → {status: 'green'|'red'|'unavailable', results:[{argv, code, ms, tailPath}]}: spawn with no shell:true, per-command timeout from the `timeout N` token, SIGTERM→SIGKILL escalation copied from reverifyAc, an isolated per-run TMPDIR and CI=1 (1218-fo-01's red suite was proven to be live-Electron/shared-/tmp contention), short-circuit on first red, tail written to `<runDir>/<slug>.gate.json`. SHADOW ONLY in this PRD: spawnJob calls runGate after the executor exits and records a `gate` object in verdicts.json; NO status is changed. queueHealth.cjs gains `computeGateCoverage(jobs, readPrdBody)` → {withGate, withoutGate, pct} so coverage is measurable (today 512 of 1395 PRDs have neither a Gate line nor a parseable AC command).
- files: src/main/lib/artifactGate.cjs, src/main/lib/definitionOfDone.cjs, src/main/scheduler.cjs, src/main/lib/queueHealth.cjs, src/main/lib/__tests__/artifactGate.test.cjs, vitest.config.ts
- deletes/simplifies: Replaces SHELL_META_RE's all-or-nothing rejection with a sequence parser, so the gate authors already write becomes machine-executable instead of being discarded.
- memories retired: none

### gate-green-unparks — A green gate with an attributable commit beats every transcript heuristic

- classes: post-run adjudication; estimate 30 min; depends on: prd-gate-spec-shadow
- problem: A run that LANDED A COMMIT but printed no PASS sentinel can still be parked on transcript pattern noise: the only rescue in src/main/runVerify.cjs requires `sentinel === 'pass' && commitEvidence`, so commit evidence alone never overrides transcript_errors. Measured over 949 verdicts sidecars: 212 parks, of which 77 are transcript_errors with NO sentinel — by construction each of those committed and was parked anyway (36% of all parks). The final-20% is_error heuristic fires on throwaway probes recovered by a different command (isSelfRecovered only matches an identical tool_use description within 30 events): live row 1218-fo-01 is parked on `is_error=true in final 20% of transcript (event 179/209)` where event 179 is a `git apply` probe, while its commit d1edf15 is on main. Parking a green job then costs a second full executor run and a slot.
- mechanism (as synthesized): Pure `adjudicateRun({gate, branchCommit, committedDuringRun, transcriptVerdict})` in artifactGate.cjs: gate green + a commit attributable to the job (sm-job/<slug> tip via branchNameFor, re-verified through the existing resolveLandedCommitEvidence) → completed with verdict `gate_green_commit_landed`, which wins over ANY transcript verdict and over a `fail` sentinel; gate green + no commit → today's commit-guard chain unchanged; gate unavailable → today's chain verbatim EXCEPT that transcript_errors/verify_unavailable are demoted to annotations whenever commit evidence exists (this alone flips the 77 measured parks). runVerify.verifyRun takes a `gate` parameter and pushes detectPattern hits and the final-20% is_error branch into `annotations`, never `issues`, when the gate is green or commit evidence exists. `gate_green_commit_landed` joins COMPLETED_EQUIVALENT_VERDICTS in src/main/lib/terminalRunOutcome.cjs. selectAutoFixTargets (scheduler.cjs:9180) early-returns false for a gate-green row so a green job can never buy a second executor run. DIRECTION LIMIT: this PRD ships the un-park direction ONLY — a red gate still falls through to today's chain and cannot newly park anything.

- DESIGN verdict: REFUTED as written — The PROBLEM is real and I reproduced its headline numbers exactly, but the MECHANISM is unsound as written on four independent counts. (1) The commit-attribution primitive does not exist at the moment it is consulted. The PRD defines "a commit attributable to the job" as "sm-job/<slug> tip via branchNameFor". In spawnJob's `finally` block, `jobWorktree.integrateJobBranch` is followed immediately by `jobWorktree.cleanupJobWorktree({ ..., keepBranch: !integration.ok })`, and `cleanupWorktree` in gitWorktree.cjs runs `git branch -D <branch>` whenever `keepBranch` is falsy. `verifyRun` is called ~190 lines LATER in the same function. So on the exact happy path this PRD targets — green run, branch integrated — `sm-job/<slug>` has already been deleted and `branchNameFor` resolves to nothing. ### ladder-collapse-regate — Collapse the six-rung needs_review ladder to three, keyed on the gate

- classes: post-run adjudication, fix-commit archaeology; estimate 30 min; depends on: gate-green-unparks
- problem: The ladder is five hand-written eligibility predicates with no completeness invariant — isExhaustedAutoFix (src/main/scheduler.cjs:8625), isGuardParkedWithoutAutoFix (:8674), isPlanUnqueued (:8703), isFixPlanDead (:8722), isUnresolvableNeedsReview (:8605) — plus NEEDS_REVIEW_RESOLVE_CAP (:9008), healRefusalReason (:8779), computeLooksDone (:9356) and a reverify pass that re-scans the SAME immutable transcript every 10 minutes and deterministically re-derives the same verdict forever (RESCANNABLE_VERDICTS, :8535). Six of the fifteen ladder commits in the last six weeks exist solely to close a gap discovered in production between two of these predicates; the live 1218-fo-01 row is the seventh instance — needs_review + transcript_errors + autoFixAttempted:true with no autoFixOutcome falls between every door.
- mechanism (as synthesized): Three rungs, all keyed on the gate rather than on verdict strings. `mechanical` (no model): selectMechanicalRecoveryTarget + selectResumeRecoveryTarget + selectLeftoverQuarantineTarget behind one selector — pure git/session work. `re-gate` (no model): periodically re-run the PRD's gate at current HEAD; green + an attributable commit → completed. `investigate`: one fix-plan, one attempt, keeping the pinned `--model` on the spawned `claude -p`. DELETE what the gate makes unnecessary: computeLooksDone (:9356), healRefusalReason (:8779) and NO_ATTRIBUTABLE_COMMIT_VERDICTS, NEEDS_REVIEW_RESOLVE_CAP (:9008) with applyNeedsReviewAutoResolve's requeue/skip cap (:9088), and shrink RESCANNABLE_VERDICTS to the gate-less fallback set so a gate-bearing row is never re-scanned against its own frozen transcript. needsReviewLedger.LADDER_RUNGS becomes ['mechanical','re-gate','investigate','manual-reset'] and classifyLadderRung maps the old source strings onto the new set so historical history.jsonl lines still reduce. Add the completeness invariant as a test: every (status × verifierVerdict × autoFix* ) tuple is matched by exactly one rung selector or appears in a documented allow-list.
- files: src/main/scheduler.cjs, src/main/lib/needsReviewLedger.cjs, src/main/lib/artifactGate.cjs, src/main/lib/__tests__/needsReviewLedger.test.cjs, src/main/__tests__/scheduler-needs-review-ladder.test.cjs
- deletes/simplifies: Deletes four named mechanisms outright (computeLooksDone, healRefusalReason, NEEDS_REVIEW_RESOLVE_CAP, applyNeedsReviewAutoResolve's cap) and cuts the ladder from six rungs to three — the only PRD in the chain whose net effect on scheduler.cjs is a line-count decrease.

## What was queued

The ss-* PRD chain (see the scheduler queue for this Epic) applies the design verifiers' amendments: fail-closed variants only, no prefix recovery on job shards, no kill authority outside the app, the six-rung ladder collapse deferred until an artifact gate exists, and every scheduler.cjs editor serialized through dependsOn so worktree merges cannot collide.
